# Augmentation review

Generated by `python -m autogate_bench.augment review`; do not edit by hand.
Items are sorted by risk, highest first. Risk is the closest call among: token
Jaccard to an OOD utterance / 0.5, to a val/test utterance /
0.7, and (when its tags differ) to the nearest existing intent /
0.5; each validation error adds 1. Above 1.0 is past a limit.

60 goals, 290 pairs; validation: 0 errors, 197 warnings.

Columns: **nearest** is the existing seed or paraphrase (non-OOD intents) with the
highest token Jaccard (rules-baseline tokenizer); **tags differ** lists the tags in
which that intent differs from the goal (for a pair side: `intent` if the nearest
utterance belongs to another intent); **OOD** and **val/test** are the highest
Jaccard to any OOD, or val/test, seed or paraphrase.

## Synthetic goals

### radio_programme_guide (risk 2.00)

- Ask what programme is on a radio station now or next
- group `media`; tags cloud_preferred / low / none / none
- rationale: broadcast programme data (RDS and DAB programme guides) arrives with the signal onboard; online schedules are fuller, so LOCAL offline

### heat_side_mirrors (risk 1.33)

- Turn on the side mirror heaters to clear water off them
- group `vehicle_control`; tags local_ok / low / safety_critical / reversible
- rationale: HVAC_SIDE_MIRROR_HEAT; droplets on the side mirrors block the rear view needed for a lane change, a visibility hazard like defrost_windshield (judgment)
- nearest existing intent (most utterances): `fold_mirrors` (3/10), tags local_ok / low / restricted / reversible **(differ)**

### send_money_to_contact (risk 1.00)

- Send money to a person by UPI
- group `communication`; tags needs_cloud / high / none / irreversible
- rationale: a UPI transfer needs the payee and amount checked on screen and a PIN typed (UX_RESTRICTIONS_NO_KEYBOARD), like pay_bill but to a person; money leaves the account
- nearest existing intent (most utterances): `send_text_message` (3/9), tags needs_small_local / low / none / irreversible **(differ)**
- **attention:** 2 warnings
